The assurance of the judges and the independence of the judiciary, above all, is to ensure the freedom of citizens. This applies both to the fact that the judiciary is the most important force limiting the execution and to the protection of the right to a fair trial. Therefore, the danger of the dismissal of supreme court judges today and that this will become a practice that can later be repeated is a danger to all of us. Despite everything, we have an obligation to defend institutions and the Constitution, to take care of them, to force state bodies to abide by the constitutional boundaries set for them. Because that's the only way we can protect our own freedom, secure our future. I thank you, I pay my respects.
